Title: Полное собрание русских летописей, томъ тринадцатый, вторая половина: I. Дополненія къ Никоновской лѣтописи; II. Такъ называемая Царственная книга (Complete Collection of Russian Chronicles, vol. 13, part 2: additions to the Nikon Chronicle for 1558-1567, and the so-called Tsar's Book)
Creator: Anonymous (the Nikon Chronicle continuation and the Tsar's Book, sixteenth-century Moscow chronicle texts); published by the Imperial Archaeographic Commission (the host record names S. F. Platonov as editor; the preface cites Platonov's 1893 study of the Tsar's Book)
Publisher: St Petersburg: Typography of I. N. Skorokhodov, for the Imperial Archaeographic Commission
Publication Date: 1906
Rights: Public Domain (published 1906, more than 95 years before 2026; the Internet Archive item carries no rights field)
Language: rus
Source: Internet Archive item polnoesobranierusskihletopisejizdannoep46, file polnoesobranierusskihletopisejizdannoep46_djvu.txt, fetched directly (curl, raw bytes) by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session, second pass, VI.23 world agent), 2026-10-23; host rights statement: the item carries no possible-copyright-status or licence field (community upload from the Presidential Library, prlib.ru item 358654, per the item's source_url); the rights basis is the 1906 imprint read on the title page in the OCR (file line 33)
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged (no characters altered, nothing retyped, dropped or corrected; the file contains 0 long-s glyphs; the OCR's doubled spaces between words are left as scanned). Rights basis restated: a book printed at St Petersburg in 1906 is public domain in the United States by date under the 95-year rule. File line numbers cited in this header are counted in the OCR text alone, before this header: add 10 to find a cited line in this file.
Content note: Language tag 'rus': sixteenth-century chronicle Russian printed in the old orthography, with the Commission's Russian preface. Order in the file: title pages, the editors' preface (from about file line 63: the chronicle text for 1558-1567 from the Synodal, Alexander Nevsky and Lebedev copies; the Tsar's Book from the illuminated Synodal manuscript), the additions to the Nikon Chronicle for 1558-1567 (ДОПОЛНЕНIЯ, from about file line 248), then the Tsar's Book (ТАКЪ НАЗЫВАЕМАЯ ЦАРСТВЕННАЯ КНИГА, from about file line 6167), the official illuminated chronicle of the years 7042-7061 (1533-1553): the death and tonsure of Vasily III, Ivan IV's minority, the fire and rising of 1547, the Kazan campaigns and the siege of 1552 with the church's prayers, under Metropolitan Macarius. OCR sampled at five points: readable Cyrillic with the yat letter misread and the variant-reading sigla garbled; check quotations against the page images. Volume 13 part 1 (the Nikon Chronicle for 1505-1558) was not found on the host: the item catalogued as it (archive.org polnoesobranierusskihletopisejizdannoep59) is a duplicate scan of volume 11.
